                                    1951+***********************************************************************
                                    1952+**                PAGE FRAME TABLE ENTRY       4/6/78                 *
                                    1953+**                                                                    *
                                    1954+**   THIS MACRO SUPPORTS: SU33                                @ZA31128*
                                    1955+**                                                                    *
                                    1956+**                                                                    *
                                    1957+**   ALL PFTES ARE CONTIGUOUS AND ARE LOCATED VIA DIRECT INDEX        *
                                    1958+**   ON THE HIGH ORDER 12 BITS OF ANY REAL ADDRESS. (THESE 12         *
                                    1959+**   BITS = PAGE FRAME NUMBER.) THE PAGE FRAME TABLE ENTRY            *
                                    1960+**   INDEX = PAGE FRAME NUMBER TIMES 16 (4 LOW ORDER BINARY ZEROS).   *
                                    1961+***********************************************************************
 000000                             1962+PFTE     DSECT , PFTEPTR
 000000                             1963+PFTPGID  DS    0F              AN IDENTIFIER OF THE VIRTUAL PAGE
                                    1964+*                              CURRENTLY OCCUPYING THIS FRAME.

                                    1966+*                              SUBDIVIDED INTO PFTASID AND PFTVBN.
 000000                             1967+PFTASID  DS    XL2 -           THE ASID OF THE PAGE CURRENTLY
                                    1968+*                              OWNING THE FRAME
 000002                             1969+PFTVBN   DS    XL2 -           VIRTUAL BLOCK NUMBER  (HIGH
                                    1970+*                              ORDER 12 BITS OF 24 BIT VIRTUAL
                                    1971+*                              ADDRESS, LEFT ADJUSTED AND PADDED WITH
                                    1972+*                              4 LOW ORDER BINARY ZEROS) CURRENTLY
                                    1973+*                              OWNING THE REAL FRAME.

 000004                             1975+PFTFQPTR DS    H -             FORWARD PAGE FRAME QUEUE POINTER

                                    1977+*                              ON THIS PFQ
